add_namespace = annex
country_event = { #Fate of Mongolia
	id = annex.7000
	title = annexation_decision_mongolia
	desc = annexation_decision_mongolia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Release Mongolia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.7000.a"
		name = annex.7000.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
				factor = 50
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MON }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MON }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = RUS
					}
				}
			}
			MON = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = RUS
				}
			}
			MON = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_RUS_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}


		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = ROC
					}
				}
			}
			MON = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
					original_tag = ROC
				}
			}
			MON = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_ROC_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #give to Republic of China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.give_to_republic_of_china"
		name = annex.give_to_republic_of_china
		trigger = {
			ROC = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ROC = {
			transfer_state = 330
			transfer_state = 321
			transfer_state = 1138
			transfer_state = 1139
		}
		330 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		321 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		1138 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		1139 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #give to Taiping
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			CHI = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		CHI = {
			transfer_state = 330
			transfer_state = 321
			transfer_state = 1138
			transfer_state = 1139
		}
		330 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		321 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		1138 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		1139 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		MON = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 50
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
				factor = 50
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Gansu
	id = annex.7001
	title = annexation_decision_gansu
	desc = annexation_decision_gansu_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Give to Mongolia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7001 option annex.7001.a"
		name = annex.7001.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			MON = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
					tag = MAN
				}
				MON = {
					is_subject_of = RUS
				}
				factor = 100
			}
		}

		MON = {
			transfer_state = 1137
			transfer_state = 297
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #give to Republic of China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.give_to_republic_of_china"
		name = annex.give_to_republic_of_china
		trigger = {
			ROC = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ROC = {
			transfer_state = 1137
			transfer_state = 297
		}
		1137 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		297 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #give to Taiping
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7000 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			CHI = {
				exists = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		CHI = {
			transfer_state = 1137
			transfer_state = 297
		}
		1137 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		297 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		1137 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
		297 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 100
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
					tag = MAN
				}
				MON = {
					is_subject_of = RUS
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
				RUS = {
					owns_state = 321
				}
				factor = 100
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Sinkiang
	id = annex.7002
	title = annexation_decision_sinkiang
	desc = annexation_decision_sinkiang_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Release Sinkiang
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.7002.a"
		name = annex.7002.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = CHI
					tag = ROC
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
				factor = 50
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SIK }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = RUS
					}
				}
			}
			SIK = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIK_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = RUS
				}
			}
			SIK = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIK_Occupied_Monarchy_RUS
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes

				}
			}

				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIK_Occupied_Republic_ROC
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #give to Republic of China
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.give_to_republic_of_china"
		name = annex.give_to_republic_of_china
		trigger = {
			ROC = {
				exists = yes
				OR = {
					owns_state = 760
					owns_state = 759
					owns_state = 287
					owns_state = 619
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ROC = {
			transfer_state = 617
			transfer_state = 618
			transfer_state = 1397
			transfer_state = 619
			transfer_state = 287
			transfer_state = 759
			#
			transfer_state = 586
			transfer_state = 589
			transfer_state = 1396
			transfer_state = 654
		}
		617 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		618 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		1397 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		619 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		287 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		759 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		#
		586 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		589 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		1396 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		654 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #give to Taiping
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7002 option annex.give_to_china"
		name = annex.give_to_china
		trigger = {
			CHI = {
				exists = yes
				OR = {
					owns_state = 760

					owns_state = 619
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}

			base = 10

			}
		}

			transfer_state = 617

			transfer_state = 759

			transfer_state = 586

country_event = { #Fate of East Turkestan
	id = annex.7003
	title = annexation_decision_east_turkestan
	desc = annexation_decision_east_turkestan_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = RUS
					original_tag = ROC
					original_tag = CHI
					original_tag = SIK
					original_tag = KUM
					original_tag = XSM
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
	}

	option = { #Release East Turkestan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7003 option annex.7003.a"
		name = annex.7003.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no

			base = 100

				}
				factor = 50
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ETR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes

						original_tag = RUS
					}
				}
			}
			ETR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes

					owns_state = 617
					owns_state = 287
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		ROC = {
			transfer_state = 617
			transfer_state = 618
			transfer_state = 1397
			transfer_state = 619
			transfer_state = 287
			transfer_state = 759
		}
		617 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		618 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		1397 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		619 = { add_claim_by = ROC }

				}
			}

				NOT = {

			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10

		RUS = {

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = KOR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = RUS
					}
				}
			}
			KOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KOR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = RUS
				}
			}
			KOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KOR_Occupied_Monarchy_RUS
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			KOR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = KOR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7012 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		KOR = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7012 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7012 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Vietnam
	id = annex.7015
	title = annexation_decision_vietnam
	desc = annexation_decision_vietnam_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Release Vietnam
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.7015.a"
		name = annex.7015.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = BFE
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BFE
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = VIN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = CHI
					}
				}
			}
			VIN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VIN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = CHI
				}
			}
			VIN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VIN_CHI_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			VIN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Reform a colonial federation
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.vietnam_colonial_fed"
		name = annex.vietnam_colonial_fed
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						tag = ENG
						tag = FRA
						tag = RUS
						tag = GER
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					country_exists = BFE
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		BFE = {
			transfer_state = 840
			transfer_state = 671
			transfer_state = 286
			transfer_state = 1119
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = RUS
				}
				RUS = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_PB_Satellite_State
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = BFE_RUS
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = GER
				}
				GER = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_PB_Satellite_State
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = BFE_GER
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = FRA
				}
				FRA = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_PB_Satellite_State
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = BFE_FRA
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = ENG
				}
				ENG = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_constituent_dominion
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				}
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Integrate it to the Indochinese holdings
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.vietnam_integration"
		name = annex.vietnam_integration
		trigger = {
			country_exists = BFE
			BFE = {
				is_subject = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = BFE
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = RUS
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = GER
							is_subject_of = ENG
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = ENG
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = RUS
							is_subject_of = FRA
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = GER
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = RUS
							is_subject_of = FRA
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = FRA
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = GER
							is_subject_of = ENG
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		BFE = {
			transfer_state = 840
			transfer_state = 671
			transfer_state = 286
			transfer_state = 1119
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7015 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes

		}

country_event = { #Fate of Laos
	id = annex.7016
	title = annexation_decision_laos
	desc = annexation_decision_laos_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Release Laos
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7016 option annex.7016.a"
		name = annex.7016.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					tag = BFE
					tag = VIN
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BFE
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = LAO }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			LAO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = LAO_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			LAO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = LAO_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #reform a colonial federation
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7016 option annex.vietnam_colonial_fed"
		name = annex.vietnam_colonial_fed
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						tag = ENG
						tag = FRA
						tag = RUS
						tag = GER
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					country_exists = BFE
					ROOT = {
						tag = VIN
						tag = BFE
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					has_subject = BFE
				}
				factor = 100
			}
		}
		BFE = {
			transfer_state = 670
			transfer_state = 1122
			transfer_state = 1418
			transfer_state = 1419
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = RUS
				}
				RUS = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_PB_Satellite_State
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = BFE_RUS
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = GER
				}
				GER = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_PB_Satellite_State
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = BFE_GER
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = FRA
				}
				FRA = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_PB_Satellite_State
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = BFE_FRA
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = ENG
				}
				ENG = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = BFE
						autonomous_state = autonomy_constituent_dominion
					}
				}
				BFE = {
					drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				}
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #integrate it to the Indochinese holdings
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7016 option annex.vietnam_integration"
		name = annex.vietnam_integration
		trigger = {
			country_exists = BFE
			BFE = {
				is_subject = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = VIN
					tag = BFE
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = RUS
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = GER
							is_subject_of = ENG
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = ENG
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = RUS
							is_subject_of = FRA
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = GER
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = RUS
							is_subject_of = FRA
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = FRA
					}
					BFE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject_of = GER
							is_subject_of = ENG
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		BFE = {

		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord

	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		536 = { retain_state = yes }
		1136 = { retain_state = yes }
		1135 = { retain_state = yes }
		533 = { retain_state = yes }
		528 = { retain_state = yes }
		1132 = { retain_state = yes }
		530 = { retain_state = yes }
		529 = { retain_state = yes }
	}

	option = { #Release Japanese Empire
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.7020.a"
		name = annex.7020.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 20
				ROOT = {
					has_government = rc
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
					OR = {
						has_government = ni
						has_government = ds
						has_government = rc
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = JAP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Release Japanese Shogunate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.7020.b"
		name = annex.7020.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_government = jc
						has_government = ss
						has_government = lb
						has_government = cn
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 60
				ROOT = {
					has_government = rc
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
					OR = {
						has_government = ni
						has_government = ds
						has_government = rc
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = JAP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		
		JAP = {
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_Shogunate
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Civilize Japan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.japanese_colonial"
		name = annex.japanese_colonial
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = RUS
				original_tag = ENG
				original_tag = GER
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 90
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
					OR = {
						has_government = ni
						has_government = ds
						has_government = rc
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 70
				ROOT = {
					tag = ENG
					OR = {
						has_government = ni
						has_government = ds
						has_government = rc
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = JAP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RUS
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_RUS_Colonial
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = ENG
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_ENG_Colonial
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = GER
				}
			}
			JAP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = JAP_GER_Colonial
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		JAP = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7020 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

			#transfer_state = 1136
			transfer_state = 1135
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Release North Japan
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7021 option annex.7021.b"
		name = annex.7021.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes

				factor = 100
				ROOT = {

					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		NJP = {
			#transfer_state = 536
			#transfer_state = 1136
			transfer_state = 1135
		}

				set_cosmetic_tag = NJP_Occupied_Republic
			}

		}

country_event = { #Fate of Siberia
	id = annex.7023
	title = annexation_siberia
	desc = annexation_siberia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	}

	option = { #Release an independent Siberia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.7023.a"
		name = annex.7023.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SIB }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			SIB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIB_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			SIB = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIB_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Reintegrate Siberia into Russia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.7023.b"
		name = annex.7023.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			RUS = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = SIB
			}
			RUS = {
				transfer_state = PREV
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		SIB = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7023 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Brunei
	id = annex.7042
	title = annexation_decision_bru
	desc = annexation_decision_bru_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	
	immediate = {
		if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    original_tag = SWK
                    AND = {
						OR = {
							owns_state = 333 #Sarawak
							owns_state = 1414 #Sabah
						}
                    }
                }
            }
            save_event_target_as = can_annex
        }
        if = {
            limit = { SWK = { ROOT_can_grant_land_ignore_ideology = yes } }
            SWK = { save_event_target_as = sarawak }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                1149 = { #Eastern Kalimantan
                    annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
                    owner = {
                        NOT = { tag = event_target:sarawak }
						OR = {
							owns_state = 333 #Sarawak
							owns_state = 1414 #Sabah
						}
                        ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
                    }
                }
            }
            var:1149.owner = { save_event_target_as = borneo }
        }
	}

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7042 option annex.7042.a"

			is_subject = no
		}

			base = 30
			modifier = {
                factor = 0
                annexations_can_annex = yes
            }

                factor = 0
                has_event_target = borneo
                OR = {
                    is_major = no
                    is_ally_with = event_target:borneo
                }
            }
            modifier = {
                factor = 0
                has_event_target = sarawak
                OR = {
                    is_major = no
                    is_ally_with = event_target:sarawak
                }
            }
		}

            base = 70

                factor = 0
                annexations_can_annex = yes
            }
            modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:sarawak }
                is_ally_with = event_target:borneo
                #annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = yes
            }
            modifier = {

            }

			}

			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}

		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate

		name = annex.integration

		}

		}
	}

	}

		trigger = {

			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = PB_Indian_Unifier
					capital_scope = {
						is_on_continent = india
					}
				}
			}
			OR = {
				MUG = {
					exists = yes
				}
				AWD = {
					exists = yes
				}
				BHP = {
					exists = yes
				}
				FOM = {
					exists = yes
				}
				RAJ = {
					exists = yes
				}
				BAN = {
					exists = yes
				}

					exists = yes
				}

					exists = yes

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		
		1099 = {
			set_state_flag = PB_Indian_Unifier_State
		}
		PB_give_to_indian_unifier = yes
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Grant it to the legitimate Chinese State
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7058 option annex.7058.b"
		name = annex.7058.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				ROOT = {				
					tag = ROC
					tag = CHI
				}
			}
			OR = {
				CHI = {
					exists = yes
					AND = {
						owns_state = 619
						owns_state = 287
						owns_state = 758
					}
				}
				ROC = {
					exists = yes
					AND = {
						owns_state = 619
						owns_state = 287
						owns_state = 758
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				ROOT = {
					has_country_flag = PB_Indian_Unifier
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					CHI = {
						exists = yes
						AND = {
							owns_state = 619
							owns_state = 287
							owns_state = 758
						}
					}
				}
			}
			CHI = {
				transfer_state = 1099
			}
			1099 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					ROC = {
						exists = yes
						AND = {
							owns_state = 619
							owns_state = 287
							owns_state = 758
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ROC = {
				transfer_state = 1099
			}
			1099 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		}
		else = {
		
		}
		
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7058 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		1099 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
	}
	
	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7058 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				ROOT = {
					tag = RAJ
					tag = BAN
				}

				}
				factor = 100
			}
			modifier = {
				tag = ROC
				factor = 100
			}
			modifier = {
				tag = CHI
				factor = 100
			}
		}

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Fate of Java
	id = annex.7059
	title = annexation_decision_1147
	desc = annexation_decision_1147_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = INS
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = { INS = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
			INS = { save_event_target_as = dutch_east_indies }
		}
	}

	option = { # Release Java Whole
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.7059.a"
		name = annex.7059.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:dutch_east_indies = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			USS = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = USS_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			USS = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = USS_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = USS }
		335 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #West Java
		1338 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Banten
		1339 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #East Java
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to INS
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.7059.b"
		name = annex.7059.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = dutch_east_indies
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_subject = event_target:dutch_east_indies
				annexations_can_annex = no
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_ally_with = event_target:dutch_east_indies
				annexations_can_annex = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:dutch_east_indies }
		335 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1338 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1339 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1147 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		335 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1338 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1339 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } 
		1147 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		335 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
		1338 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
		1339 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } 
		1147 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7059 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

	id = annex.7068

		1259 = { retain_state = yes }
	}

	option = { # Release Nepal
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7068 option annex.7068.a"
		name = annex.7068.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 40
				ROOT = {
					tag = TIB
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = NEP }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			NEP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = NEP_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {

					has_republican_government = yes
				}
			}
			NEP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = NEP_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		NEP = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate

		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0

				factor = 100

			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 60
				ROOT = {
					tag = TIB
				}
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7068 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { # Fate of Timor
	id = annex.7073
	title = annexation_decision_1148
	desc = annexation_decision_1148_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				original_tag = INS
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = { INS = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
			INS = { save_event_target_as = dutch_east_indies }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { USS = { exists = yes } }
			USS = { save_event_target_as = java }
		}
	}

	option = { # Give Timor to INS
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7073 option annex.7073.a"
		name = annex.7073.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = dutch_east_indies
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:dutch_east_indies }
                is_ally_with = event_target:java
            }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = java }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 60
				is_ally_with = event_target:dutch_east_indies
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:dutch_east_indies }
		1148 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } # Timor
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { # Give Timor to USS (Java)
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7073 option annex.7073.b"
		name = annex.7073.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = java
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				add = 60
				has_subject = event_target:java
			}
			modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:java }
                is_ally_with = event_target:dutch_east_indies
            }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = dutch_east_indies }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 60
				is_ally_with = event_target:java
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:java }
		721 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } # Timor
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { # Integrate Timor directly
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7073 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

	}

			}
			var:1353.owner = { save_event_target_as = papua }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {

					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:673.owner = { save_event_target_as = sulawesi }
		}
	}

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				add = 60
				has_subject = event_target:papua
			}
			modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:papua }
                is_ally_with = event_target:sulawesi
            }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = sulawesi }
			}
			modifier = {

				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

			limit = {
				CHI = {
					owns_state = 601
				}
			}

				transfer_state = 434
			}
			434 = { add_claim_by = CHI }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROC = {
					owns_state = 601
				}
			}
			ROC = {
				transfer_state = 434
			}
			434 = { add_claim_by = ROC }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Give it to Nepal
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7090 option annex.7090.c"
		name = annex.7090.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NEP = {
				exists = yes
				owns_state = 324
			}
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						tag = NEP
						tag = ROC
						tag = CHI
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

			transfer_state = 434
		}
		434 = { add_claim_by = NEP }
	}

		434 = { transfer_if_owned = yes }
	}

	option = { #Integrate

		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100

					tag = NEP
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100

				}
			}

country_event = { #Fate of Lampung
	id = annex.7096
	title = annexation_decision_1340
	desc = annexation_decision_1340_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    original_tag = DEL
					original_tag = HAR
					original_tag = ACE
					original_tag = INS
					original_tag = KRA
                }
            }
            save_event_target_as = can_annex
        }
		if = {
            limit = {
                672 = { # Palembang
                    annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
                    owner = {
                        ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
                    }
                }
            }
            var:672.owner = { save_event_target_as = palembang }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                1355 = { # Krakatoa Archipelago
                    annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
                    owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:palembang }
                        ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
                    }
                }
            }
            var:1355.owner = { save_event_target_as = krakatoa }
        }
	}

	option = { #Grant Lampung to Palembang
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7096 option annex.7096.a"
		name = annex.7096.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = palembang
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:palembang }
                is_ally_with = event_target:krakatoa
            }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = krakatoa}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:palembang = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:palembang }
		1340 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Lampung
	}
	
	option = { #Grant Lampung to Hawk a toa
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7096 option annex.7096.b"
		name = annex.7096.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = krakatoa
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:krakatoa }
                is_ally_with = event_target:palembang
            }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = palembang}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:krakatoa = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:krakatoa }
		1340 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Lampung
	}	

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7096 option name annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		1340 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Lampung
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7096 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		1340 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Lampung
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7096 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	after = {
        recheck_annexations = yes
    }
}

country_event = { # Fate of Krakatoa
	id = annex.7097
	title = annexation_decision_1355
	desc = annexation_decision_1355_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Asia

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					1338 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Banten
					1340 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } # Lampung
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				1340 = { # Lampung
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1340.owner = { save_event_target_as = lampung }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				1338 = { # Banten
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:1338.owner = { save_event_target_as = banten }
		}
	}

	option = { # Grant the state to Lampung
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7097 option annex.7097.a"
		name = annex.7097.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = lampung
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:lampung }
				is_ally_with = event_target:banten
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = banten }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:lampung = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:lampung }
		1355 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Krakatoa Archipelago
	}

	option = { # Grant the state to Banten
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7097 option annex.7097.b"
		name = annex.7097.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = banten
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:banten }
				is_ally_with = event_target:lampung
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = lampung }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:banten = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:banten }
		1355 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } # Krakatoa Archipelago
	}

	option = { # Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.7097 option name annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
